# Retired research milestone

## Current direction — REXWHICHKEY-20261031-072107

User-directed replanning: which-key behavior is the product; Rex is a documented API dependency, not a research milestone. Stop further tests and experiments. Do not launch probes, benchmarks, test runners, GUI exercises, remote trials or temporary config/reload procedures. This revision schedules none, including at release. Existing results and untested limitations remain accurately recorded, not converted into passes. Product behavior and safety requirements remain; previous experimental/RFC-before-implementation barriers are superseded. Existing test files are preserved. This planning update does not execute implementation or approve provisional IF-* signatures.

This milestone is cancelled, not completed. No further terminal, geometry, routing, configuration-probe, remote or tested-RFC research is scheduled. Existing results, failures, reviews, resource identities and untested findings are preserved in native history. Remove this milestone as a prerequisite of all product phases. No cleanup or Rex state changes are authorized. Product requirement coverage belongs to implementation phases 2–6, not research completion.

{"attempt":11,"content":"{\"schemaVersion\":1,\"kind\":\"research-audit\",\"recordRef\":\"phases/rex-whichkey-01-design-gates.md\",\"specHash\":\"sha256:beeb25180e3a5249ce20c14f84d7352bcac927f19b9effa2f1d1aeef253d148f\",\"runId\":\"rex-whichkey-phase1-research-audit-20261030-1\",\"sourceIdentity\":{\"head\":\"d0ac58193614de2b9fbc45b3333b0850ec6c65b5\",\"tree\":\"8043f777cc2acdf050555bb3fe7df65a0692a9a4\",\"sourceUnchanged\":true},\"outcome\":\"corrections-needed\",\"terminalFindings\":[{\"claim\":\"The pinned x/ansi printable-ASCII shortcut splits e + U+0301 in both width modes.\",\"status\":\"confirmed\",\"evidence\":[\"/Users/curtbushko/go/pkg/mod/github.com/charmbracelet/x/ansi@v0.11.7/parser_decode.go:167-170 returns one printable ASCII byte, width 1, consumed length 1.\",\"DecodeSequence and DecodeSequenceWc both call decodeSequence; FirstGraphemeCluster is reached only after the ASCII shortcut.\",\"FirstGraphemeCluster at lines 435-450 uses uax29 segmentation, then separate WcWidth and GraphemeWidth calculations.\",\"uax29/v2@v2.7.0/graphemes/iterator.go:80 onward only takes its ASCII shortcut when the next byte is ASCII or absent; an immediately following combining mark falls back to segmentation.\"],\"correction\":\"The root-cause trace is sound for contiguous ASCII-base clusters. Reusing FirstGraphemeCluster is a plausible experimental correction, not a verified fix. REXWHICHKEY-20261030-120047 remains Open.\"},{\"claim\":\"Ultraviolet loses the detached mark on this probe's rendering path.\",\"status\":\"confirmed\",\"evidence\":[\"/Users/curtbushko/go/pkg/mod/github.com/charmbracelet/ultraviolet@v0.0.0-20260703014108-f5a850f9c2b7/styled.go:116-119 selects the affected decoders.\",\"styled.go:130-171 writes positive-width cells and resets the temporary cell; line 199 accumulates the detached mark in that temporary cell.\",\"The next positive-width sequence overwrites temporary Content at line 134.\",\"The probe puts the label before a newline and subsequent footer text, providing that overwrite.\",\"Bubble Tea v2.0.8 cursed_renderer.go:311 calls content.Draw on its cell buffer.\"],\"correction\":\"Do not generalize this to every zero-width sequence being immediately discarded. styled.go:213 onward can write a trailing nonempty temporary cell at the current cursor position, not attach it to the preceding base. Dangling marks, line-building, bounds and subsequent draws require separate assertions.\"},{\"claim\":\"Forcing mode 2027 cannot correct the ASCII split.\",\"status\":\"confirmed\",\"evidence\":[\"Both decoder modes share the unconditional ASCII return.\",\"Bubble Tea v2.0.8 tea.go:794-796 changes the renderer width method after a supported Unicode-core report.\",\"cursed_renderer.go:690-702 enables mode 2027 and changes cellbuf.Method, without bypassing the decoder shortcut.\"],\"correction\":\"Retain negotiated supported/unsupported mode coverage; neither forced 2027 nor unchanged Bubble Tea is an accepted solution.\"},{\"claim\":\"The proposed decoder regressions are useful but cannot establish general Unicode correctness or identical widths in both modes.\",\"status\":\"confirmed\",\"evidence\":[\"x/ansi parser_decode.go:435-450 dispatches WcWidth to go-runewidth and GraphemeWidth to displaywidth.\",\"go-runewidth@v0.0.23/runewidth.go:208 onward measures a cluster using its first positive-width rune.\",\"displaywidth@v0.11.0/width_test.go:60-61 specifies width 2 for VS16 keycaps; its width.go:205 onward backs off the final ASCII byte before non-ASCII content.\",\"Ultraviolet styled.go handles positive widths 1 through 4, while buffer.go:40-99 manages wide-cell placeholders and partial overwrites.\"],\"correction\":\"Assert complete sequence bytes, consumed byte count, next state and exact remaining suffix for string and []byte inputs in both modes. Minimum matrix: e + U+0301, multiple marks, Ae + mark + B, adjacent marked bases, pure ASCII, CJK, keycaps with/without VS16, non-ASCII ZWJ emoji and ASCII followed by ZWJ. Expect e + U+0301 width 1 and consumed length 3; define mode-specific keycap expectations rather than requiring equal widths. Verify subsequent cell coordinates, wide placeholders and redraw/overwrite behavior, not just retained bytes. Non-ASCII ZWJ cases are controls for existing behavior, not evidence that the ASCII patch fixed them.\"},{\"claim\":\"An ASCII segmentation patch alone covers control boundaries and detached combining marks.\",\"status\":\"disputed\",\"evidence\":[\"x/ansi treats ESC/control sequences separately before printable segmentation.\",\"Ultraviolet styled.go preserves style/link state but does not merge a later zero-width sequence into an already-written base cell.\",\"styled.go's end-of-loop flush targets the current cursor position.\"],\"correction\":\"Add characterization cases for leading isolated marks, trailing marks, e followed by SGR then U+0301, OSC-8 boundaries, newline/carriage return, wrapping and clipping. Specify intended behavior before broadening the patch. Preserve control semantics and exact decomposed content; do not normalize or silently drop marks to make tests pass. Separately supplied chunks cannot be assumed to form one decoder cluster.\"},{\"claim\":\"The retained strict Unicode harness has unsafe artifact handling and a premature capture boundary.\",\"status\":\"confirmed\",\"evidence\":[\"docs/experiments/rex-validation/tui/internal/probe/unicode_test.go:15-18 waits for RESEARCH READY then overwrites /tmp/rex-whichkey-validation/unicode-{candidate}.vt.\",\"internal/probe/pty_test.go:65 onward returns once the marker appears in accumulated chunks.\",\"internal/probe/probe.go places the Unicode label and footer after that marker.\",\"docs/experiments/rex-validation/results/unicode-acceptance.log retains direct=true and tea=false with a strict tea failure.\"],\"correction\":\"First authorize a harness-only change: use isolated temporary artifacts, wait for a complete later frame/footer boundary, and deliberately test split reads after READY. The retained failure is historical evidence, not a fresh result. Raw-byte containment does not prove visible glyph widths, cell placement or redraw correctness. PTY regressions must identify the rebuilt experimental binary and effective dependency replacement so stale retained binaries cannot supply a false green.\"},{\"claim\":\"The direct probe mistakes OSC replies for Escape; a buffered reader is a reasonable prerequisite but fragmentation correctness remains unproved.\",\"status\":\"confirmed\",\"evidence\":[\"docs/experiments/rex-validation/tui/cmd/direct/main.go reads one byte and maps byte 27 immediately to esc.\",\"internal/probe/reply_test.go explicitly accepts cancel for direct, making it diagnostic rather than an acceptance test.\",\"Ultraviolet decoder.go:763-840 recognizes OSC 11 and BEL/ST termination.\",\"terminal_reader.go:267 onward buffers unknown/incomplete input and delays ambiguous short ESC input.\"],\"correction\":\"Require acceptance tests that observe choose after a reply followed by y, and no intermediate key/cancel dispatch. Cover every split point, BEL, both bytes of ST, standalone Escape, bounded timeout, Tab/Backspace, resize, error and restoration. Do not claim EventDecoder called on individual bytes provides buffering.\"},{\"claim\":\"Fragmenting the two-byte ST terminator exposes an additional source-level risk in the proposed input approach.\",\"status\":\"inference\",\"evidence\":[\"Ultraviolet decoder.go:763 onward returns ignoredEvent when an OSC terminator ESC arrives without a following backslash.\",\"terminal_reader.go scanEvents handles ignoredEvent by clearing the event rather than treating it as unknown/incomplete.\"],\"correction\":\"Include a deterministic split immediately after the OSC terminator ESC. {"attempt":10,"content":"{\"schemaVersion\":1,\"kind\":\"planning-proposal\",\"role\":\"planner\",\"runId\":\"rex-whichkey-p01-planner-a10\",\"attempt\":10,\"recordRef\":\"phases/rex-whichkey-01-design-gates.md\",\"specHash\":\"sha256:e338c6d957cd37f52387af2eaad02c119557924590ae4db0330c9e76cb208e67\",\"outcome\":\"precise-contract-and-permission-gaps-established\",\"approvalStatus\":\"Recommendations only; no new permission or acceptance inferred.\",\"sourceIdentity\":{\"taskRef\":\"tasks/rex-whichkey-01-02-graphical-routing.md\",\"taskSpecHash\":\"sha256:5e5db4c684475552b17e0ce8b1b740d365222de69232553be218e4dd7c277863\",\"reviewEventId\":\"rex-whichkey-p01-t02/review/1\",\"head\":\"b499ddb3a4b8a6774e5c7678411ccea6a99d6596\",\"tree\":\"e6403cfd81ec22147fc5ba4d5c55b2df7bde71f8\",\"worktreeClean\":true,\"reviewReadViaNativeMtn\":true},\"answer\":{\"supportedFullRouteUnderCurrentRestrictions\":false,\"conclusion\":\"No documented existing primitive was established that supplies both genuine app-origin invocation evidence and an instrumentable nonempty configuration-action queue lifecycle without changing loaded configuration or the app's scripts. This is a bounded permission/observability gap, not evidence that Rex universally lacks app-origin actions or deferred queues.\",\"missingPrimitive\":\"An additive, temporary, instrumentable configuration action callable from the existing app without config reload, or an already-installed benign action exposing origin context and nonempty queue success/failure behavior. Neither is established on this server.\",\"existingUserResourcesUnchanged\":true},\"verifiedCapabilities\":[{\"capability\":\"Exact current versions and connection provenance\",\"finding\":\"Server 0.0.1792787366-g623b2d uses API revision 7; installed CLI 0.0.1793359647-g966596 uses revision 8. Server PID13408 started 2026-10-24T15:06:38.663415Z, exit_empty=false. The supplied host/instance match. The sole listed app is the authorized client, connected since 2026-10-30T08:13:14.322746-04:00, Unix peer uid501/PID31813, Rex Beta0.1.0(1670). App metadata is self-reported; the principal is server-identified.\",\"evidence\":[\"rex version\",\"Explicit-endpoint whoami, server.status, client.inspect and client.list\",\"server config generation24/hash8fd37a982c758f891010cc795ec2ae6488336c243ef73895e67bf0b6a5417caa\"]},{\"capability\":\"Real app configuration-action entry\",\"finding\":\"Existing configuration actions can be invoked through app keys or its command palette. client.action operates as its requesting connection, has no client_id override, checks local attachment/block membership, and returns result plus ordered client_actions. Its origin field is caller-supplied and is not proof of a physical app event.\",\"evidence\":[\"Current server: rex api describe client.action --json, input_schema and validation_notes\",\"https://www.superlogical.com/rex/docs/customize/actions#the-run-function\",\"https://www.superlogical.com/rex/docs/customize/actions#running-an-action\"]},{\"capability\":\"Key forwarding is not arbitrary app impersonation\",\"finding\":\"client.key acts on its own attached connection. It invokes Lua function bindings, then forwards unhandled input to the block; named actions are not dispatched by sending their shortcut to this method. client.keymap describes function bindings as client.key.forward. The keymap is always the requesting client's; client_id is rejected. A CLI keymap query cannot inspect or replace the existing app's complete merged keymap.\",\"evidence\":[\"Current server: client.key validation_notes\",\"Current server: client.keymap validation_notes\",\"Installed rex keymap --help\"]},{\"capability\":\"No advertised app action executes arbitrary Lua/configuration actions\",\"finding\":\"The authorized app advertises pane.send_key and client.key.forward, but no generic Lua evaluator, configuration-action invoker, script installer, or disconnect action. pane.send_key promises terminal input, not arbitrary shortcut dispatch. client.key.forward refers to the key that performed it; its metadata does not establish a synthetic-event API. Neither is a documented substitute for a genuine app-origin invocation.\",\"evidence\":[\"Current authorized-app client.inspect/actions metadata\",\"Installed rex client --help\"]},{\"capability\":\"Queue and invocation boundaries\",\"finding\":\"Configuration-action queues run after run returns and are discarded on failure. Script queues execute immediately. rex.invoke preserves its invocation context; a script-local action remains script execution, while an undefined script action name delegates to the server. Neither operation upgrades a nominated client into authenticated app origin. Raw CLI client.action returns client_actions to that CLI connection, not automatically to the app.\",\"evidence\":[\"/tmp/rex-whichkey-research/lua.md:145-169\",\"/tmp/rex-whichkey-research/actions.md:170-208\",\"Current server: client.action output_schema\",\"https://discord.com/channels/1546797889009881158/1562120012179841065, discussion20-25\"]},{\"capability\":\"Exact-client delivery, not launch authentication\",\"finding\":\"client.perform authenticates the requester from its connection and returns performed_by equal to the requested target. Supplied origin/session/block values are forwarded, not authenticated as launch context. Missing targets return not_found; reconnecting/disconnecting targets return client_unavailable. Timeout does not cancel delivery and must not trigger blind retries.\",\"evidence\":[\"Current server: client.perform input_schema and validation_notes\"]},{\"capability\":\"Script installation cannot be remotely scoped with -C\",\"finding\":\"client.set_script replaces all scripts of its requesting client across attached sessions; client_id and session_id are rejected. CLI script bindings do not reach the app keymap. No documented additive app-script installation route was found.\",\"evidence\":[\"Current server: client.set_script input_schema and validation_notes\",\"/tmp/rex-whichkey-research/lua.md:27-47\"]}],\"existingActionAssessment\":{\"liveMetadata\":\"Only cycle_focus at init.lua:36 and pane_width at init.lua:83 were listed as configuration actions.\",\"targetedInspection\":[\"The on-disk cycle_focus definition delegates to a detached navigation script and can navigate sessions; it is not a bounded queue probe. Its helper was not inspected or invoked.\",\"The on-disk pane_width definition returns a resize_pane result and contains no client.queue. Existing app shortcuts could demonstrate an app-triggered resize, but not context return or nonempty queue/error-discard semantics.\"],\"limitation\":\"Live metadata identifies the action sources; targeted disk inspection is not byte-for-byte proof of the server's loaded closures. No safe existing action satisfying the complete experiment was established.\"},\"proposals\":[{\"reference\":\"1a\",\"label\":\"◇ Proposal\",\"finding\":\"T02-R001\",\"state\":\"Open\",\"recommendation\":\"Retain the precise blocker. If actual queue validation is required now, seek narrowly scoped permission for one temporary additive test action in current init.lua and two controlled reloads: install and restore. This is the least invasive documented route established, but expressly outside current permission.\",\"boundedTest\":[\"First fix R004/R005 and review fake-only identity/membership negatives; do not reuse the frozen unsafe live routes.\",\"The test action rejects any client/session/block outside the explicitly authorized fixture before mutation and records ctx.client_id/session_id/block_id/origin.\",\"Invoke it through a real key or palette interaction in the authorized app, not rex do, client.perform with nominated context, or a CLI origin flag.\",\"Use context-only, successful-queue and queue-then-error branches. Record action entry, queue calls, pre-return state/result or error, then observe only bounded owned app effects after completion. Failure must produce no queued app effect.\",\"Keep popup-removal/current-origin-focus/callback ordering separately observable; do not infer it from an earlier context snapshot.\",\"Restore only the owned temporary addition after checking for concurrent config changes; verify the original loaded hash and unchanged server instance.\"],\"authProvenance\":\"Actual app request connection supplies ctx.client_id; corroborate that ID with its server-identified Unix peer principal. Origin text alone remains insufficient.\",\"boundaries\":\"No app-script replacement, app disconnect or server restart is needed. Configuration generation and all clients' keymap notifications necessarily change on reload; this option cannot be described as leaving loaded user configuration untouched.\",\"currentPermissionAlternative\":\"Document contracts and retain R001 unmet; another manual-client script run would not close it.\"},{\"reference\":\"2a\",\"label\":\"◇ Proposal\",\"finding\":\"T02-R002\",\"state\":\"Open\",\"recommendation\":\"To keep the existing app connected, obtain a second independently disconnectable genuine graphical app client on this same server. No such client is currently listed. A second session, CLI declaring kind=app, or another window of the existing app is not a substitute.\",\"permissionNeeded\":\"Explicit authorization for a separate owned real app connection and its bounded disconnect/reconnect, using a normal supported connection on an available device. No isolated local instance/profile/socket startup procedure is established or proposed.\",\"boundedTest\":[\"Retain appA as connected observer; identify appB and capture a real appB-origin invocation before disconnection. Full origin-route acceptance depends on resolving R001.\",\"Gracefully quit only appB through its normal app UI; verify appB actually left while appA and the same server instance remain connected.\",\"Dispatch once to the captured exact appB identity. Require not_found after departure, or client_unavailable if the observed state is lost; never substitute appA or retry an uncertain timeout.\",\"Verify no chosen effect or fallback on appA and no changes outside owned resources. Reconnect appB normally and record whether its identity resumed or changed.\",\"A separately controlled dropped-transport test would be needed to exercise the lost/resumption window; graceful quit alone must not be labeled that test.\"],\"evidence\":[\"Current server client.list: one app\",\"Current client.list schema: lost clients retained up to60seconds\",\"Current client.perform validation_notes\",\"https://discord.com/channels/1546797889009881158/1547349572299395172/1550235127920201779\"]},{\"reference\":\"2b\",\"label\":\"? Validate\",\"finding\":\"T02-R002\",\"state\":\"Open\",\"assessment\":\"Ordinary current-app Quit/reopen is a supported app lifecycle, distinct from server restart, but does not satisfy the requirement that the existing app stay connected and is not recommended under current permission.\",\"survivalEvidence\":[\"Documentation states sessions run whether or not any client is looking: https://www.superlogical.com/rex/docs/automate/cli#taxonomy.\",\"Current server predates the current app connection by six days and has exit_empty=false.\",\"Community confirmation that quitting/reopening leaves the server running: https://discord.com/channels/1546797889009881158/1560740466834538538.\",\"Preview16 reports a fix for a macOS server-lifetime bug: https://discord.com/channels/1546797889009881158/1547349572299395172/1561835096070164622.\"],\"supportedProcedureIfSeparatelyAuthorized\":\"Record server instance/PID and owned terminal process identities; use normal Cmd+Q, not close-session/window, server stop, update or restart; verify the same server and terminal processes through explicit-endpoint CLI with autostart=false; reopen the existing installed app normally and verify reattachment. Stop on any unexpected server change.\",\"limitation\":\"Persistence is documented and supported by current observations, not guaranteed harmless for this exact binary/environment. The changelog fix was not independently mapped to g623b2d. No documented current client-disconnect API/action exists; public CLI prose mentioning force disconnect is not implemented-command evidence.\"},{\"reference\":\"3a\",\"label\":\"◇ Proposal\",\"finding\":\"T02-R003\",\"state\":\"Open\",\"recommendation\":\"Authorize exactly one additional disposable destination session on the pinned server; retain the existing owned session as origin. This is sufficient for meaningful delegated-picker selection without unrelated sessions.\",\"boundedTest\":[\"Create only after approval and retain returned IDs.\",\"From the original owned session, open session.switch, filter to the unique destination, visually verify the unselected owned row, cancel and verify the origin remains displayed.\",\"Repeat and select the destination; verify its actual displayed pane/window, not merely session attachment metadata. Switch back and clean up only the new owned destination after verification.\",\"Compare with the retained delegated session.switch baseline; no enumerated WhichKey session submenu or production mapping system is required.\"],\"currentPermissionLimit\":\"Same-session selection is genuine partial evidence, not cross-session parity. Do not borrow the team session or unrelated sessions.\"}],\"reviewCorrectionsAndGates\":{\"reviewAssessment\":\"R001-R003 are supported findings; no basis was found to waive them. Community reports that session.select scripting is solved address delivery convenience, not authenticated originating-app binding.\",\"routineTaskFixes\":[\"R004: validate actual invocation client before any close/focus/queue; missing or mismatched context must produce zero mutations.\",\"R005: require exact floating-layer kind and complete authorized transient membership before destructive close.\",\"R006: map session-picker parity to REQ-010 and origin/focus/routing to REQ-011; preserve historical evidence and publish corrections through the coordinator.\"],\"currentTaskBlockers\":\"R001-R003 plus R004-R006; no live rerun before safety fixes.\",\"laterProductValidation\":\"Production mapping integration, general trusted Go-to-Lua transport, and universal theme/size behavior are not newly required task02 implementations.\",\"retainedSeparateGates\":\"REQ-013 real remote validation, final tested RFC synthesis, explicit fingerprint-bound design approval, reviewed integration and combined-target verification remain outstanding. A second client connecting to this local server would not itself satisfy real-remote acceptance.\"},\"validation\":{\"performed\":[\"Read installed worker contract and Rex skill/current CLI guide.\",\"Read exact current task and review through native mtn; verified supplied phase fingerprint.\",\"Verified clean frozen builder HEAD/tree; read route.lua, route_live.lua, graphical README.md and VALIDATION-A01.md.\",\"Used explicit-endpoint, autostart=false API describe/list, whoami, status, client metadata, configuration-action metadata and filtered keymap metadata.\",\"Read targeted public Rex/Lua documentation, relevant changelog/community discussions and only the two identified action definitions.\"],\"limitations\":[\"No action invocation, key injection, graphical test, source/config/.tasks write, reload, restart, disconnect, agent launch, resource creation or integration occurred.\",\"No provider, gamingrig or remote endpoint queries occurred.\",\"No new graphical, remote, authentication or task-acceptance pass is claimed.\",\"No public documentation claim was assumed to override the current server's schemas.\"]}}\n","coordinatorQualifications":[{"assessment":"Coordinator cap was not an explicit user hard limit. Existing approved cross-session picker criterion/current-server owned-resources permission plus continue now supported one additional owned destination and actual distinct-session observations. See picker-resource-clarification/1 and picker-parity/1; no config/app-disconnect permission inferred.","topic":"One-session operating cap"},{"assessment":"Read-only metadata confirms ~/.config/rex/init.lua is a non-writable Nix-store symlink. Direct append is not viable. A temporary symlink-safe copy/restore probe with global config reloads requires separate explicit approval; no store or upstream edits performed.","topic":"Current config storage"},{"assessment":"Bounded follow-up docs found advertised session.open_on_web points to a web client not published for testers (feedback1560414321316593765). No web client was opened or assumed available; a real second client remains unestablished.","topic":"Possible web client"}],"eventId":"rex-whichkey-p01-planner-a10/proposal/1","gateEligible":false,"kind":"historical","provenance":"Fresh read-only mtn-rex planner, exact openai-codex/gpt-6.1-sol high. {"attempt":3,"content":"Open proposal, not approved: permit one temporary, guarded configuration-action probe on the current Rex server to validate genuine app-origin context and deferred configuration-action queue success/error ordering.\n\nThe current ~/.config/rex/init.lua is a non-writable symlink into /nix/store. Never edit/chmod that store object, change the upstream Nix configuration, activate a generation, or perform a permanent ownership migration. If explicitly approved, first preserve the exact symlink target and original bytes/metadata, prepare a verified writable temporary copy with one uniquely named test action/module, and validate it with the documented non-mutating rex config check path. The action must reject every client/session/block outside the owned test fixtures before mutation. Source safety findings R004/R005 must be fixed and tested before any live route is reused; R006 requirement labels must be corrected without overwriting historical evidence.\n\nTemporarily substitute only that prepared copy at the configuration path, perform a controlled configuration reload, and invoke the guarded test action from the real app through its normal key/palette path. Observe actual server-supplied client/session/block context, popup absence/current-origin focus, callback execution/return, successful queued effect after configuration-action completion, and no queued effect after a queue-then-error branch. Do not substitute script-mode immediate queues or nominated CLI origin flags.\n\nThen restore the exact original symlink and perform the restore reload, verifying the test action is gone, original configuration metadata is restored and server identity is unchanged. Check for concurrent file/config changes before restore and never overwrite unexpected user edits. Keep all backups and evidence. This changes the loaded configuration globally and resets configuration runtime generations; restoring the file cannot recreate the prior Lua VM state. It is not a server restart. No existing app-script replacement, unrelated resource mutation, Nix-store write, Nix activation, final design adoption or phase2–6 authority is requested.\n\nThis proposal is an explicit exception request to the existing no-config-reload boundary. The user's earlier current-server/new-session permission and subsequent continue do not approve it. A separate genuine graphical-client disconnect/no-fallback environment remains an unmet gate; the current app stays connected unless separately authorized otherwise.\n","decisionId":"REXWHICHKEY-20261030-215048","decisionState":"Open","eventId":"rex-whichkey-p01-coordinator-a03/config-probe-permission-proposal/1","gateEligible":false,"kind":"historical","notApproved":["temporary init symlink override","config install/restore reloads","existing app disconnect","Nix store/upstream edits"],"provenance":"Actual task02 review R001 and targeted read-only planner-a10 contract investigation; current init metadata confirms non-writable Nix-store symlink. {"attempt":12,"content":"{\"schemaVersion\":1,\"kind\":\"planning-proposal\",\"role\":\"planner\",\"runId\":\"rex-whichkey-p01-planner-a12\",\"attempt\":12,\"recordRef\":\"phases/rex-whichkey-01-design-gates.md\",\"specHash\":\"sha256:e338c6d957cd37f52387af2eaad02c119557924590ae4db0330c9e76cb208e67\",\"outcome\":\"preliminary-non-gating-rfc-candidate\",\"provenance\":{\"parentRunId\":\"rex-whichkey-p01-coordinator-a03\",\"worktree\":\"/Users/curtbushko/workspace/github.com/curtbushko/rex-whichkey/main\",\"collection\":\"/Users/curtbushko/workspace/github.com/curtbushko/rex-whichkey/main/.tasks\",\"model\":\"openai-codex/gpt-6.1-sol\",\"thinking\":\"high\",\"modelEvidence\":\"Launch contract specifies the exact provider/model and thinking level; PI_MODEL reports gpt-6.1-sol. No provider request or configuration change was made.\",\"piSessionId\":\"rex-whichkey-p01-planner-a12\",\"rexSessionId\":\"session:01a18e8f-6232-7255-9911-991096a685d5\",\"rexWindowId\":\"window:01a18fc6-ff78-788f-b8c0-a55978d3a982\",\"identityNote\":\"Pi identity and Rex resource IDs are separate; existing identities were retained.\",\"head\":\"1deb79a9bcbc297875b855e317a26dc61aff7bf3\",\"tree\":\"60ad3602c14dd70f178d49f4ebc6a5d27131bc20\",\"phaseFingerprintVerified\":true,\"inputs\":[{\"ref\":\"prds/rex-whichkey.md\",\"specHash\":\"sha256:2179140873bca50f43022deea7d262ce148ba87a2052d296ecf7fc84ee6852db\"},{\"ref\":\"designs/rex-whichkey-interface-proposal.md\",\"specHash\":\"sha256:183491c5928ca8cc8c8c4c8e1a8721c2d1fef351ab484709e09fd9ec5c054298\"},{\"ref\":\"designs/rex-whichkey-decision-register.md\",\"specHash\":\"sha256:a854fbd8bf9bf945dad4e96f923acae2c6dd786681beb0714472d939ddd33ffb\"},{\"ref\":\"designs/rex-whichkey-research.md\",\"specHash\":\"sha256:e0ff3acbc8f4df5f55d35eca62647afceca007edbebacfe80001775b246a5ad4\"},{\"ref\":\"tasks/rex-whichkey-01-03-interface-rfc.md\",\"specHash\":\"sha256:9a6e3cfc3f9daeecd941fb52a1468905181df76da75576055b021988274daa77\",\"nativeStateObserved\":\"planned\",\"prerequisites\":[\"tasks/rex-whichkey-01-01-terminal-candidate.md\",\"tasks/rex-whichkey-01-02-graphical-routing.md\"]}],\"frozenTask02Source\":{\"head\":\"b499ddb3a4b8a6774e5c7678411ccea6a99d6596\",\"checkout\":\"../rex-whichkey-p01-t02-builder-a01\",\"review\":\".mtn-tmp/a03/graphical-current-server/reviewer-result.json\",\"historicalPlanner\":\".mtn-tmp/a03/graphical-current-server/planner-a10-result.json\"},\"method\":\"Read worker/RFC guidance, exact native task brief and fingerprints, registered documents, retained review/planner results, frozen routing source and existing schemas/documentation. No mutable builder-a02 checkout was read.\"},\"candidateRfc\":\"# Preliminary RFC candidate: Rex WhichKey\\n\\nStatus: WIP, non-gating synthesis under existing Open proposal REXWHICHKEY-20261030-121541. This text neither starts nor delivers task03, establishes a tested final RFC, adopts a public interface, nor requests user approval. Contract details below remain proposals until exact user approval.\\n\\n## 1. Authority, scope and evidence\\n\\n**1a — ✓ Agreed baseline:** A fresh, short-lived Go executable at `./cmd`, relatively flat application code, minimal Lua launcher, and one concrete `rex` package with `Client`, `Session`, `Window`, `Block` and generic `Call`. Executable callbacks remain in trusted persistent Rex Lua. Preserve root Taskfile/lint policy. No daemon, service hierarchy, generic hexagonal scaffolding, SSH/WOL/bootstrap or session-attach expansion.\\n\\n**VERIFIED, bounded evidence:** Task01's pinned nested terminal experiment is integrated at `1deb79a`, review/4 APPROVED; this is not framework adoption. Task02's frozen `b499ddb` has genuine sampled five-position/offset/opacity, client-owned resize and standalone Go-popup evidence, but review/1 is CHANGES_NEEDED and source remains unintegrated. Subsequent coordinator picker-parity/1 establishes actual distinct-owned-session picker cancel/select. REQ-010 covers picker/built-in parity; REQ-011 covers origin/focus/context.\\n\\n**1b — ✓ Agreed scheduling correction:** REXWHICHKEY-20261030-221324 moves genuine disconnected-origin/no-fallback testing to mandatory final end-of-phases release validation. It is deferred/untested, not a Phase1 prerequisite, pass or cancellation. No second app is required now. Rex API state and action results are authoritative; do not request redundant screenshots for those facts. Unrun behavior remains unverified.\\n\\nHistorical statements in the interface/research documents about incomplete terminal delivery, untested picker parity, repaired registration, custom-server prerequisites or present disconnected-app gates must be read with their later corrections, not reinstated.\\n\\n## 2. Source 10a: launch, data-only dispatch and callback lifetime\\n\\n**2a — ◇ Proposal, IF-LAUNCH/IF-DISPATCH:** Keep only invocation bookkeeping and executable configuration in persistent Lua. Go owns menu interaction, rendering and confirmation, not callback execution. Lua retains the original callback/action, authoritative arguments and captured origin; the menu receives display/navigation data and opaque entry references.\\n\\nCandidate snapshot fields are protocol version, runtime generation, invocation reference, deadline and bounded entries containing keys, display attributes, entry IDs and confirmation requirements. Candidate selection fields are protocol version, invocation reference, choose/cancel discriminator, entry reference when choosing and explicit confirmation. Reject extra executable code, arbitrary action names, replacement arguments, paths and target overrides. These are candidate product fields, not published Rex schemas.\\n\\nProposed state progression is `Reserved → Open → Consumed | Cancelled | Expired | Invalidated`. Reserve capacity before creating resources. Validate schema, invocation authority, generation, expiry, entry eligibility, confirmation and current condition before consuming. Recheck immediately before consumption after any yielding validation. Consume before callback or other selected side effect; callback failure never restores an invocation. Replay and same-invocation reentrancy fail. Cancellation performs cleanup without the chosen action. Release callback references on terminal states.\\n\\nRetain the earlier candidate limits only as unapproved fixture parameters: 120-second invocation age, 16 live invocations/runtime, one live menu/origin app-block, 1 MiB snapshot, 64 KiB selection and 4096 entries. Use an injected clock in fixtures. Lazy expiry bounds stored entries but does not establish prompt physical popup cleanup. A supported proactive expiry/cleanup mechanism still needs validation. Removed/unknown references must stay invalid; a runtime generation must not recycle old authority after reload.\\n\\n**2b — ? Validate, implementability-critical:** Persistent configuration actions and CLI-to-Lua data-only callbacks were demonstrated for CLI-origin experiments. That does not establish a complete graphical-origin return path. Configuration-action `rex.client.queue` is deferred until return and discarded on failure; script-mode queues execute immediately. Calling a configuration dispatcher from a new CLI connection does not, merely by passing a saved context table, make its queue belong to the original app. `client.action` returns `client_actions` to its requesting connection. `client.perform` delivers declared app actions to an exact client, but the inspected app catalog does not establish a generic configuration-action/callback invoker. The production app-bound callback/queue transport therefore remains Open.\\n\\nThe proposed lifecycle is: capture trusted launch context; create the terminal layer; return promptly; accept one selection into a controller that survives popup destruction; consume; verify exact owned popup removal; resolve the origin's current window and focus; execute the selected callback/dispatch; observe the applicable queue outcome. Do not make the popup process close itself and then depend on another RPC from that killed process. The complete handoff, acknowledgement and deferred-queue ordering are unverified. The 31-second historical popup demonstrates terminal-owned lifetime, not this complete ordering.\\n\\n**2c — ◇ Proposal, origin and authority:** Bind endpoint/server instance, runtime generation, app client, stable session/block and returned popup IDs in retained state. Launch-time window is placement data, not block identity. API-reported state is trusted; caller-nominated `origin`, client/session/block fields are not upgraded to authenticated launch provenance. Establish the actual requesting app connection at launch and a return path authorized for that invocation. A client equality check is necessary fixture ownership safety, not production launch authentication.\\n\\nThe existing proposal's Go `crypto/rand` helper called through documented `rex.exec(argv, opts)` is a candidate entropy source, not a tested transport. Fail closed on helper/entropy failure. Argv bearer tokens are not confidential from same-user observers or administrators. Owner-only server-local files or private channels remain Open until their creation, persistence, transfer and invocation paths are documented and exercised. `rex.exec` stdin support does not imply stdin injection into a newly created terminal block. No production capability registry is required to finish task02's bounded experiment.\\n\\n## 3. Source 10b: configuration and source/data boundaries\\n\\n**3a — ◇ Proposal, IF-CONFIG:** Use distinct integration/user module names, for example `rex_whichkey` and `whichkey_user_config`. Both inline `setup(table)` and `setup(require(user_module))` supply one table. Defaults fill absent values; no implicit inline/file merge. Reject duplicate setup unless explicit replacement semantics are later approved. Validate completely before publishing callbacks/state; preserve the previous working configuration on rejected replacement where replacement is supported.\\n\\nLua compiles conditions, inherited attributes, callback references and authoritative action arguments; Go receives only their bounded menu projection. Dynamic labels and conditions execute in trusted Lua, not in Go. Sanitize terminal controls in display data without silently normalizing away Unicode. Real isolated sibling `require` worked historically; the proposed setup API, failure atomicity, installed search paths and remote loading remain unverified.\\n\\n## 4. Source 10f: concrete Rex calls and CLI parsing\\n\\n**4a — ◇ Proposal, IF-REX:** `Client.Call(ctx, method, params, out)` centralizes process execution, explicit endpoint, JSON encoding/decoding and bounded diagnostics. `Session` carries client plus stable session ID; `Window` and `Block` retain that session identity. Resolve current containment using `session.view`, not invented `session.windows`, `window.blocks` or cached window-derived session IDs.\\n\\nRetained method contracts support `session.list {}`, `session.view {session_id}`, `session.new_layer {session_id, window_id?, bounds, layout, focus?}`, `session.resize_layer {session_id, layer_id, bounds}`, `session.close_layer {session_id, layer_id}`, `session.focus_block {session_id, block_id}` and `block.method {session_id, block_id, method, args}`. New-layer results contain `layer_id`, `block_ids`, `revision`; focus defaults false. Bounds are `[x,y,w,h]`, not right/bottom coordinates. Keep app actions separate from server methods; `client.action` cannot directly execute `pane.split`.\\n\\nUse an argument array equivalent to `rex --autostart=false --server ENDPOINT api call METHOD JSON_OBJECT`. The retained CLI guide documents one JSON-object argument, `@file` or `-` stdin; this is not key=value parsing. Prefer encoded inline JSON for ordinary bounded calls, or the documented stdin form when explicitly implemented. Never split JSON on whitespace or interpolate a shell. Resource wrappers supply explicit `session_id` and reject contradictory targeting rather than relying on ambient defaults. Generic Call retains its explicit low-level parameters; it is not an inferred app-authority API.\\n\\nUse `os/exec`, `context`, `encoding/json`, `errors` and a small private runner function seam, not an exported transport framework. Require a complete bounded JSON result with no trailing document; preserve method/target/stage and bounded stderr in errors. Distinguish pre-execution failure from an unknown post-submission outcome. Timeout, cancellation, disconnect or undecodable success output after a mutation must never cause automatic retry. `client.perform` timeout explicitly does not cancel possible later execution; `performed_by` identifies the requested executing client, not authenticated launch provenance.\\n\\n## 5. Sources 10c, 10e and 10g: layers, parity and terminal choice\\n\\n**5a — ◇ Proposal, IF-TUI:** Prefer native fractional layers based on the retained bounded graphical samples. Do not copy the old full-window overlay without a demonstrated current defect. Before closing a layer, validate its exact floating kind and complete authorized transient block set; reject mixed/protected membership. Consume before uncertain cleanup and never retry close blindly. If the origin moved, resolve its current window within the captured session; if missing, stop rather than searching unrelated sessions.\\n\\nThe script experiment needed explicit app window selection as well as server focus. In a configuration action, a queued `session.select` is deferred, so the script's observed ordering cannot simply be copied as proof of pre-callback app selection. This belongs to the open lifecycle contract, not another screenshot requirement.\\n\\nPreserve delegated `session.switch` picker behavior. Distinct-session cancel/select is now evidenced; an enumerated WhichKey session submenu is not required parity. Other built-in actions still need their own implementation/evidence.\\n\\nBubble Tea v2.0.8 with the exact locally corrected pinned ANSI/Ultraviolet experiment is a supported candidate for final comparison, not an accepted dependency/fork. Historical unchanged Bubble Tea Unicode and minimal-direct-I/O terminal-reply failures remain provenance; later task01 delivery supersedes the terminal experiment's earlier blocked status. Neither terminal delivery nor the standalone Go popup proves complete themes, mappings, remote behavior or production callback integration. Keep pure menu state in ordinary flat Go code; no framework choice implies interface/service layers.\\n\\n## 6. Source 10d and remote compatibility\\n\\n**6a — ◇ Proposal, IF-HOSTS:** Use `rex hosts --json` effective inventory, preserving stable IDs, nested host data, source/held flags, cached-home status and warnings. The command merges local/home inventory; `--server` and `REX_SERVER` do not retarget it. Raw `hosts.list` is not an interchangeable merged result. Proposed ownership is the menu process host's effective inventory, clearly described to users; that user-visible choice remains Open. Listing never initiates deferred attach/bootstrap flows.\\n\\nLua configuration/callbacks and the terminal executable/helper must exist where the target server executes them. Do not copy a graphical client's socket or absolute temporary/Nix path into a remote runtime contract. Real remote menu/config/helper/callback and endpoint propagation remain separate unmet validation; a second app on this local server would not satisfy them.\\n\\n## 7. Offline work, rollout and remaining gates\\n\\n**7a — ◇ Proposal:** Once native prerequisites and admission permit task03 fixtures, use its exclusively owned `docs/experiments/rex-validation/contracts` area for small failing-first schema, argv, selection and lifecycle models. Favor stdlib implementations and existing assertion conventions; do not alter root dependencies, Taskfile/lint policy, terminal modules or another worker's graphical files. Fake models prove rejection/order logic only, not Rex runtime transport or app queue behavior.\\n\\nThe currently admitted task02 correction lane can fix R004/R005 ownership safety and R006 traceability offline; this planner neither duplicates that work nor treats its pending fixes as verified. Task03 still waits for delivered task01/task02 and native admission. Required later checks include fresh independent combined review, serialized integration, combined-target verification, remaining tested contracts, real remote validation and exact fingerprint-bound final-design approval. Phases2–6 are not authorized by this draft.\\n\\nIntended rollback is disabling the new launcher/import and invalidating outstanding invocations, without taking ownership of the user's init.lua or retrying uncertain actions. Excluded or unsupported alternatives remain executable Lua serialization, held-open human-interaction actions, fixture IDs as authentication, sole-client fallback, shell interpolation, destructive retries and architecture scaffolding. Preserve their evidence/history without inventing user rejection.\\n\\nDisconnected-origin/no-fallback remains mandatory only at the final release gate under REXWHICHKEY-20261030-221324. No new live experiment, permission round or infrastructure setup is requested by this candidate.\",\"verifiedEvidence\":[{\"classification\":\"VERIFIED_THIS_LEAF\",\"subject\":\"Current phase and task03 identity\",\"evidence\":\"Native fingerprint matches the launch phase hash. Existing permission is not evidence of execution.\"},{\"ref\":\"2c\",\"state\":\"Open\",\"question\":\"Which launch fields are connection-derived provenance, and how is the selection authority bound to that captured app/session/block across a separate CLI request?\",\"resolution\":\"Use retained schemas to specify the trust boundary and spoofing negatives; later validate actual launch provenance. Do not use caller origin text or ID equality alone.\"},{\"ref\":\"2a\",\"state\":\"Open\",\"question\":\"What supported controller/handoff survives popup destruction and establishes removal, current-origin focus, callback and deferred-queue ordering?\",\"resolution\":\"Specify the acknowledgement boundary and failure ordering; test model invariants offline, then validate the actual runtime path. Script-immediate queues do not settle this.\"},{\"ref\":\"2c-transport\",\"state\":\"Open\",\"question\":\"What working persistence/invocation path carries the snapshot and invocation authority, and what same-user disclosure boundary is intended?\",\"resolution\":\"Demonstrate entropy-helper execution and either an explicitly bounded argv bearer design or a documented private server-local channel. File/stdin alternatives remain unproved.\"},{\"ref\":\"2a-expiry\",\"state\":\"Open\",\"question\":\"How are physical popup resources reclaimed after crashes/expiry without retaining callbacks indefinitely or replaying uncertain cleanup?\",\"resolution\":\"Choose measured limits and validate supported timer/event/process cleanup ownership, reload invalidation and partial-failure behavior. Lazy expiry alone is insufficient proof of physical cleanup.\"},{\"ref\":\"3a\",\"state\":\"Open\",\"question\":\"Are one-table/default-only precedence, duplicate-setup rejection and atomic configuration publication the intended public configuration contract?\",\"resolution\":\"Exercise inline/import/error fixtures and later installed runtime loading; retain as a proposal for the eventual exact design approval.\"},{\"ref\":\"6a\",\"state\":\"Open\",\"question\":\"Should the host menu show the menu-process host's effective Rex inventory, and how are executable/module/helper/endpoint paths established on a real remote server?\",\"resolution\":\"Specify inventory ownership explicitly; test preserved inventory fixtures offline and the execution/path contract on actual remote infrastructure later.\"},{\"ref\":\"5a\",\"state\":\"Open\",\"question\":\"Which terminal dependency strategy should the final design adopt after the successful pinned correction experiment?\",\"resolution\":\"Assess maintained-version/local-correction consequences using existing results. This planner does not execute or duplicate it.\",\"completionBoundary\":\"Fresh review is required; no pending fix is credited here.\"},{\"work\":\"Concrete Call argv and JSON fixture\",\"cases\":[\"Exact explicit endpoint, autostart=false, method and single JSON-object argument.\",\"Spaces, quotes, Unicode, newlines and shell metacharacters remain data.\",\"Explicit session injection and contradictory wrapper target rejection.\",\"Empty/truncated/invalid JSON, trailing document, oversized output, nonzero exit and bounded stderr.\",\"Cancellation before start versus timeout/decoding failure after submission; no automatic mutation retry.\"],\"approach\":\"Small stdlib exec/context/JSON implementation with a private runner function; an os.Args helper-process test can exercise argv without launching Rex.\",\"admission\":\"Task03 contracts-only fixture ownership after delivered task01/task02 prerequisites, native admission and an exact coordinator dispatch. Product rex package implementation belongs to later approved Phase2 scope, not this preliminary draft.\"},{\"work\":\"Data-only wire and invocation-state model\",\"cases\":[\"Version/shape/size rejection; choose/cancel exclusivity; missing confirmation.\",\"Unknown entry, attempted action/args/target substitution and stale generation.\",\"Expiry boundary with fake clock, capacity rejection before resource allocation and per-origin overlap limit.\",\"Changed condition, consume-before-callback, replay, reentrancy, cancellation, callback error after side effect and reload invalidation.\",\"Unknown post-submit outcome never reopens or retries the invocation.\"],\"approach\":\"Pure table-driven Go fixtures using encoding/json and injected clock/effect functions; no exported registry/service hierarchy.\",\"admission\":\"Same prerequisite-bound task03 contracts fixture lane; numerical bounds remain candidate test parameters.\",\"completionBoundary\":\"A Go model cannot prove trusted Lua state, entropy transport, queue delivery or graphical launch authentication.\"},{\"work\":\"Lifecycle model and recorded-schema resource fixtures\",\"cases\":[\"New-layer IDs recorded before later operations; missing/partial result treated explicitly.\",\"Exact floating membership check before destructive close.\",\"Moved-origin lookup preserves session identity; missing origin stops dispatch.\",\"Close failure, focus failure, controller death and callback exception suppress later selected effects.\",\"No second RPC is required from a popup process after its layer is destroyed.\"],\"approach\":\"Reuse retained session/view/layer schema shapes and effect journals; keep script-immediate and configuration-deferred queue models distinct.\",\"admission\":\"Task02 owns its existing safety fixtures now; any new cross-contract model waits for admitted task03 and stays inside contracts ownership.\"},{\"work\":\"Configuration projection and host inventory fixtures\",\"cases\":[\"One explicit table, absent-only defaults, duplicate setup and rejected-publication preservation.\",\"Executable/function-valued data never crosses the Go wire.\",\"Control-sequence sanitization preserves intended Unicode data.\",\"Nested hosts[].host shape, stable IDs, source/held flags, cached-home warnings and local duplicate-label behavior.\",\"No assumption that --server retargets effective inventory.\"],\"approach\":\"Recorded data fixtures and candidate-contract assertions, not a new Lua interpreter dependency or live Rex call.\",\"admission\":\"Task03 may validate bounded candidate contracts only after native prerequisites/admission.
